- **understand-colbert_late_interaction**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.colbert_late_interaction` **DEFINITION:** Core architectural principle deferring interaction between query and document token representations until scoring time, enabling efficient per-token similarity computation without full cross-encoding of query-document pairs. - **understand-colbert_maxsim_operator**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.colbert_maxsim_operator` **DEFINITION:** Maximum similarity operator that for each query token finds the highest-scoring document token via cosine similarity, then aggregates across query tokens to produce document relevance score. - **understand-colbert_token_embedding**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.colbert_token_embedding` **DEFINITION:** Dense contextualized vector representation produced per token by an encoder model, preserving token identity and positional context for late interaction scoring. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_enterprise_integration_patterns/understand-cp_purge_action) · ★1)
- **understand-cp_purge_policy**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_message_channel.cp_purge_policy` **DEFINITION:** A set of rules defining which messages qualify for purging: age threshold, retry exhaustion, poison detection criteria, or pattern matching on content. - **understand-cp_purge_trigger**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_message_channel.cp_purge_trigger : deep_message(6), enterprise_integration_patterns(3)` **DEFINITION:** A condition that initiates a channel purge cycle; may be time-based, count-based, or event-based such as threshold_exceeded. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_enterprise_integration_patterns/understand-cp_purge_trigger) · ★1)
- **understand-cp_stale_message**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_message_channel.cp_stale_message` **DEFINITION:** A message that has exceeded its relevance window or idle duration and no longer carries actionable data for downstream consumers. - **understand-cp_threshold_condition**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_message_channel.cp_threshold_condition : deep_message(6), enterprise_integration_patterns(3)` **DEFINITION:** A predicate over channel_state_snapshot values that evaluates to true when purge_trigger should fire, such as depth_overflow or stale_age_exceeded. - **understand-creational_pattern**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.creational_pattern` **DEFINITION:** A design pattern category focused on object creation mechanisms, abstracting the instantiation process so that code remains independent of the specific classes being created or how they are assembled. - **understand-djt_wave_attenuation**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.djt_wave_attenuation` **DEFINITION:** The emergent property that decorrelated jitter reduces the probability of simultaneous retries among competing threads; achieved through per-thread history-dependent ranges that diverge over time. - **understand-lactobacillus**: **CALL NUMBER:** `sourdough_baking.lactobacillus : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(6)` **DEFINITION:** Bacterial strains producing lactic and acetic acids that create sourdough tanginess ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lactobacillus) · ★1)
- **understand-lactobacillus_esterase_activity**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_fermentation.lactobacillus_esterase_activity : deep_lactobacillus(6), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** Enzymatic capability of lactobacillus strains to catalyze esterification reactions between organic_acids and ethanol during sourdough fermentation ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lactobacillus_esterase_activity) · ★1)
- **understand-ladder**: Load the full AI Maturity Ladder philosophy into context for deeper understanding of graduation levels and decision criteria ([tychay/ai-maturity-ladder](https://github.com/tychay/ai-maturity-ladder/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-ladder))
- **understand-lapp_flavor_output_signature**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lapp_flavor_output_signature : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(7), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The characteristic ratio of fruity_ester_notes and floral_ester_notes (from lactate esters) versus sharp vinegar-like nuances (from acetate esters) in the final sourdough aroma profile resulting from lapp_pathway_allocation_ratio. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lapp_flavor_output_signature) · ★1)
- **understand-lapp_lactate_bias**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lapp_lactate_bias : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(7), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The enzymatic state wherein lactobacillus_esterase_activity exhibits lower Km and higher turnover for lactic_acid over acetic_acid, directing condensation flux through le_lactate_ester_pathway to yield lactate ester dominance. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lapp_lactate_bias) · ★1)
- **understand-lapp_pathway_allocation_ratio**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lapp_pathway_allocation_ratio : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(8), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The proportional distribution of esterification catalysis between le_lactate_ester_pathway and le_acetate_ester_pathway as determined by the relative kinetic favorability of each acyl donor substrate. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lapp_pathway_allocation_ratio) · ★1)
- **understand-lapp_ph_modulation**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lapp_ph_modulation : sourdough_baking(3), deep_fermentation(3), deep_lactobacillus(1)` **DEFINITION:** The influence of dough pH on the ionization state of organic_acid substrates, affecting their availability as le_organic_acid_substrate and thus modulating lactobacillus_esterase_activity acyl donor preference. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lapp_ph_modulation) · ★1)
- **understand-lch_forward_reverse_rate_ratio**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lch_forward_reverse_rate_ratio : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(8), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The instantaneous comparison of le_kinetic_properties for esterification versus hydrolysis determining whether net accumulation of fc_estolide_compounds occurs at any fermentation moment. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lch_forward_reverse_rate_ratio) · ★1)
- **understand-lch_hydrolysis_reaction**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_le_activity.lch_hydrolysis_reaction : deep_fermentation(29), deep_lactobacillus(9), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The reverse chemical reaction wherein water molecule attacks the ester bond of fc_estolide_compounds, cleaving them back into le_ethanol_substrate and le_organic_acid_substrate, releasing the water_byproduct consumed in condensation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-lch_hydrolysis_reaction) · ★1)
- **understand-le_acetate_ester_pathway**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_acetate_ester_pathway : deep_fermentation(29), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The specific esterification route wherein lactobacillus_esterase_activity catalyzes the reaction of acetic_acid with ethanol to form acetate esters contributing to sharp, vinegar-like aromatic nuances in sourdough headspace. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_acetate_ester_pathway) · ★1)
- **understand-le_activity**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_activity : deep_fermentation(29), deep_le_activity(13), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** Enzymatic capability of lactobacillus strains to catalyze esterification reactions between organic_acids and ethanol during sourdough fermentation, producing ester compounds that contribute to flavor_complexity. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_activity) · ★1)
- **understand-le_acyl_donor_preference**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_acyl_donor_preference : deep_fermentation(29), deep_le_activity(9), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The substrate specificity of lactobacillus_esterase_activity determining whether lactic_acid or acetic_acid serves as the primary acyl donor, influencing the resulting ester profile. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_acyl_donor_preference) · ★1)
- **understand-le_estolide_formation**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_estolide_formation : deep_fermentation(29), sourdough_baking(5), deep_le_activity(4)` **DEFINITION:** The biochemical process by which lactobacillus_esterase_activity joins organic_acid acyl groups with ethanol alcohol groups through condensation, yielding fc_estolide_compounds and water as products. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_estolide_formation) · ★1)
- **understand-le_lactate_ester_pathway**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_lactate_ester_pathway : deep_fermentation(29), sourdough_baking(5)` **DEFINITION:** The specific esterification route wherein lactobacillus_esterase_activity catalyzes the reaction of lactic_acid (derived from pyruvate reduction) with ethanol to form lactate esters contributing to fruity_ester_notes and floral_ester_notes. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_lactate_ester_pathway) · ★1)
- **understand-le_organic_acid_substrate**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_organic_acid_substrate : sourdough_baking(3)` **DEFINITION:** Carbon-chain acid substrates (lactic_acid and acetic_acid) from lactobacillus metabolism that serve as acyl donors in esterification reactions catalyzed by lactobacillus_esterase_activity. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_organic_acid_substrate) · ★1)
- **understand-le_physiological_conditions**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_physiological_conditions` **DEFINITION:** The environmental parameters affecting lactobacillus_esterase_activity including dough pH, fermentation temperature, substrate concentration, and fermentation duration that modulate catalytic efficiency. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_physiological_conditions) · ★1)
- **understand-le_substrates**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_lactobacillus.le_substrates : sourdough_baking(3), deep_fermentation(2)` **DEFINITION:** The molecular reactants consumed during lactobacillus_esterase_activity: organic_acids and ethanol that undergo esterification catalysis. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-le_substrates) · ★1)

- **understand-mcs_lock**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.mcs_lock` **DEFINITION:** Queued lock where each waiting thread spins on a locally-owned node linked into a queue; cache-friendly with only O(1) bus traffic per lock acquisition; named after Mellor-Crummey and Scott. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-row_level_locking) · ★1)
- **understand-rr_definition**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.rr_definition` **DEFINITION:** An isolation_level ensuring that all reads within a transaction see a consistent snapshot of the database as of transaction start; prevents non_repeatable_read by acquiring and holding read locks on accessed rows until transaction end. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-rr_definition) · ★1)
- **understand-rr_read_lock**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.rr_read_lock` **DEFINITION:** A shared_lock acquired when a row is read under repeatable_read; held until transaction commits or rolls back, preventing any concurrent transaction from acquiring a write_lock on that row. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-rr_read_lock) · ★1)
- **understand-rrf_fusion_score**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.rrf_fusion_score` **DEFINITION:** The sum of reciprocal rank values for a given document across all contributing rank lists; the document's position in the fused ranking is determined by descending fusion score. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_retrieval_augmented_generation_architecture_patt/understand-rrf_fusion_score) · ★1)
- **understand-rrf_rank_position**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.rrf_rank_position` **DEFINITION:** The integer position of an item within a rank list, starting at 1 for the highest-scoring result; used as the denominator in reciprocal rank computation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_retrieval_augmented_generation_architecture_patt/understand-rrf_rank_position) · ★1)
- **understand-scrap-issues**: Fetch the most recent maintainer-scrap issues (up to 3) and understand their content ([dyoshikawa/rulesync](https://github.com/dyoshikawa/rulesync/tree/HEAD/.rulesync/skills/understand-scrap-issues) · ★1.5k)
- **understand-sdna**: **WHAT:** Gnostic agent workflow DSL with LangGraph as native execution substrate. Ariadne (threading) + Poimandres (generation) = SDNA spiral. ([sancovp/sdna](https://github.com/sancovp/sdna/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-sdna))
- **understand-second_brain**: **CALL NUMBER:** `personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra.second_brain : deep_second_brain(3), deep_knowledge_management(2)` **DEFINITION:** A methodology and digital system for capturing, organizing, and retrieving knowledge externally so the biological brain can focus on synthesis and creativity rather than storage. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-second_brain) · ★1)
- **understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_atomic_visibility` **DEFINITION:** The guarantee that all threads observe the effects of a sequentially consistent operation at the same logical point in the global total order; reads observe the most recent write in the total order. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility) · ★1)
- **understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility_happens_before_cutoff_visibility**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_atomic_visibility_happens_before_cutoff_visibility : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(2)` **DEFINITION:** A seq_cst read cannot observe a write that is ordered after it in the total order; the happens-before relationship established by seq_cst operations enforces a cutoff beyond which writes are not visible. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility_happens_before_cutoff_visibility) · ★1)
- **understand-seq_cst_default_atomic_ordering**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_default_atomic_ordering : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_happens_before_relat(7), concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(3)` **DEFINITION:** The default memory ordering for std::atomic operations in C11/C++11 when no memory_order is explicitly specified; provides the strongest guarantees without requiring explicit fence code. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_default_atomic_ordering) · ★1)
- **understand-seq_cst_global_total_order**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_global_total_order : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_happens_before_relat(7), concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(3)` **DEFINITION:** A single total order of all sequentially consistent operations that is visible and agreed upon by all threads in the system; the interleaving point where all threads observe the same sequence of operation ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_global_total_order) · ★1)
- **understand-seq_cst_indivisible_atomicity**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_indivisible_atomicity` **DEFINITION:** The property that each sequentially consistent operation appears indivisible and instantaneous to all observers; no intermediate states are visible during the operation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_indivisible_atomicity) · ★1)
- **understand-seq_cst_synchronization_protocol**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_synchronization_protocol : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(1)` **DEFINITION:** The protocol by which sequentially consistent operations establish synchronization points between threads; creates a happens-before relationship across thread boundaries. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_synchronization_protocol) · ★1)
- **understand-sequentially_consistent**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.sequentially_consistent : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_c11_memory_model(16), deep_happens_before_relat(7)` **DEFINITION:** The strongest memory ordering requiring a single total order of all sequentially consistent operations visible to all threads; implied by default for std::atomic operations in C++. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-sequentially_consistent) · ★1)
- **understand-sha1_hash**: **CALL NUMBER:** `git_internals_and_the_object_model.sha1_hash : deep_commit_object(8), deep_tree_object(8)` **DEFINITION:** 160-bit cryptographic hash computed over object content; serves as object's unique addressable name in git's content-addressable store. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-sha1_hash) · ★1)
- **understand-skills**: WHAT: Native Claude Code skills — how they work, what they give/don't give you WHEN: Understanding or creating skills ([sancovp/twi-jobworld](https://github.com/sancovp/twi-jobworld/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-skills))
- **understand-slash-commands**: WHAT: Claude Code slash commands — user-initiated entry points WHEN: Understanding or creating slash commands ([sancovp/twi-jobworld](https://github.com/sancovp/twi-jobworld/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-slash-commands))
- **understand-slip_box**: **CALL NUMBER:** `personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra.slip_box : deep_knowledge_management(2)` **DEFINITION:** Another name for Zettelkasten; emphasizes the slip-box metaphor of individual cards that can be recombined freely. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-slip_box) · ★1)
- **understand-slip_card**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_knowledge_management.slip_card : personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra(11)` **DEFINITION:** A single note card serving as the atomic unit of a slip-box; the fundamental indivisible element that carries one idea. - **understand-split_brain**: **CALL NUMBER:** `distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols.split_brain : deep_consensus(7), deep_raft(6)` **DEFINITION:** Scenario where network partition causes multiple nodes to believe they are leader; resolved by quorum. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-split_brain) · ★1)
- **understand-statement_level_read_consistency**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_isolation_level.statement_level_read_consistency` **DEFINITION:** guarantee within read_committed that each SQL statement sees all changes committed at the moment that statement began execution ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-statement_level_read_consistency) · ★1)
- **understand-stream_log**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_log` **DEFINITION:** An immutable append-only sequence of events retained on disk or equivalent durable storage; unlike pubsub_message which is ephemeral, events in a stream_log persist for the configured retention period and can be reread from any offset by any consumer group. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_log) · ★1)
- **understand-stream_offset**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_offset` **DEFINITION:** A monotonically increasing integer position marker assigned to each event within a partition, uniquely identifying the event's place in the partition's sequence and enabling consumers to resume reading from a specific point. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_offset) · ★1)
- **understand-stream_partition**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_partition` **DEFINITION:** A distinct, ordered slice of an event stream, identified by a partition key or index, enabling parallel production and consumption; each partition maintains its own offset sequence independently of other partitions. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_partition) · ★1)
- **understand-structural_pattern**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.structural_pattern` **DEFINITION:** A design pattern category focused on composing classes and objects into larger structures, defining relationships between entities to build flexible and efficient hierarchies. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_design_patterns/understand-structural_pattern) · ★1)

- **understand-subtree_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tree_object.subtree_mode` **DEFINITION:** Mode 040000; entry points to a nested tree object representing a directory; the tree entry name contains a forward slash but the slash is not stored in the entry itself. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-subtree_mode) · ★1)

- **understand-symlink_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tree_object.symlink_mode` **DEFINITION:** Mode 120000; entry points to a blob object containing the target path as raw bytes; the blob content is the symlink destination, not the link data itself. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-symlink_mode) · ★1)
- **understand-synchronizes_with**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.synchronizes_with : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_happens_before_relat(7), deep_c11_memory_model(1)` **DEFINITION:** A relation between atomic operations in memory models where a release fence on one thread synchronizes with an acquire fence on another thread, establishing happens-before order across threads. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-synchronizes_with) · ★1)
- **understand-synchronizes_with_absence**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.synchronizes_with_absence : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(2)` **DEFINITION:** A relaxed atomic operation does not synchronize with any other operation; it establishes no happens-before or synchronizes-with relationship with any thread, including the thread that performed the operation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-synchronizes_with_absence) · ★1)

- **understand-tagged_union_type**: **CALL NUMBER:** `type_systems_for_working_programmers.tagged_union_type : deep_tagged_union_type(23)` **DEFINITION:** Discriminated union with explicit tag field enabling exhaustive pattern matching. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_type_systems_for_working_programmers/understand-tagged_union_type) · ★1)

- **understand-tbox**: **CALL NUMBER:** `knowledge_graphs_and_ontologies.tbox` **DEFINITION:** The terminological box — the schema-level component of a knowledge base defining classes, properties, and their relations. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_knowledge_graphs_and_ontologies/understand-tbox) · ★1)
- **understand-te_capturing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_capturing` **DEFINITION:** The first phase of the CODE framework where information is quickly captured before it fades from working memory; a frictionless gateway that accepts raw input without requiring immediate structuring. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_capturing) · ★1)
- **understand-te_distilling**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_distilling` **DEFINITION:** The process of extracting essential insights from source material through progressive summarization; a compression operation that retains meaning while reducing volume. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_distilling) · ★1)
- **understand-te_expressing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_expressing` **DEFINITION:** The final phase of the CODE framework where accumulated knowledge becomes tangible output; the conversion operation that transforms internal knowledge structures into external artifacts. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_expressing) · ★1)
- **understand-te_organizing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_organizing` **DEFINITION:** The structuring operation that transforms captured material into a retrievable system; grouping related content by semantic relationship to enable future discovery and connection. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_organizing) · ★1)

- **understand-the-news**: Focus mode for Understand The News ([glowingkitty/OpenMates](https://github.com/glowingkitty/OpenMates/tree/HEAD/backend/apps/news/focus_modes/understand-the-news) · ★46)
- **understand-theory**: Extract mathematical formulation, statistical models, and theoretical foundations from a bioinformatics method paper ([yyw-informatics/kb-skills-bioinformatics](https://github.com/yyw-informatics/kb-skills-bioinformatics/tree/HEAD/codex/skills/understand-theory) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **understand-thundering_herd_contention_surge**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_contention_surge` **DEFINITION:** A transient spike in the rate of lock or resource acquisition attempts triggered by mass wakeup, producing queue buildup and wasted retry cycles. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_contention_surge) · ★1)
- **understand-thundering_herd_retry_coordination**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_retry_coordination` **DEFINITION:** The phenomenon where concurrently woken threads retry a failing operation in near-lockstep, reinforcing the contention surge; mitigated by jitter applied to retry delays. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_retry_coordination) · ★1)
- **understand-thundering_herd_wait_set**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_wait_set` **DEFINITION:** A collection of threads blocked on a single synchronization point awaiting a condition; the structural prerequisite for thundering-herd because coordinated wakeup of this set is the failure mode. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_wait_set) · ★1)
- **understand-thundering_herd_wakeup_event**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_wakeup_event` **DEFINITION:** A single signal or broadcast that causes ALL members of a wait_set to transition from blocked to runnable simultaneously; the atomic cause of the thundering-herd pattern. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_wakeup_event) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_checkpoint_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_checkpoint_record` **DEFINITION:** A special log_record written periodically by a checkpoint_writer; records the volatile database state at a consistent point, bounding the recovery_manager's replay horizon. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_checkpoint_record) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_flush_to_disk**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_flush_to_disk` **DEFINITION:** The act of transferring the contents of tl_log_buffer to durable storage; triggered by transaction commit, tl_log_buffer overflow, or a periodic checkpoint; O(1) or O(n) bounded by buffer size. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_flush_to_disk) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_log_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_log_record` **DEFINITION:** The fundamental unit of a transaction_log: a single append-only entry recording one atomic change or lifecycle event made by a transaction; identified by an lsn. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_log_record) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_recovery_manager**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_recovery_manager` **DEFINITION:** The subsystem that, on system restart, reads the transaction_log from the last tl_checkpoint_record forward; replays committed tl_redo_log entries and rolls back uncommitted transactions using tl_undo_log. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_recovery_manager) · ★1)
